Background ==\n\nA rematch for the [[ONE Strawweight World Championship|ONE Interim Strawweight Muay Thai Championship]] between former champions [[Prajanchai P.K.Saenchaimuaythaigym|Prajanchai P.K.Saenchai]] and [[Sam-A Gaiyanghadao]] served as the event headliner.{{Cite web |title=Sam-A to rematch Prajanchai at ONE Friday Fights 22 |url=https://asianmma.com/sam-a-to-rematch-prajanchai-at-one-friday-fights-22/ |access-date=2023-05-24 |website=asianmma.com|date=24 May 2023 }} The pair previously faced each other in August 2021 at [[2021 in ONE Championship#ONE Championship: Battleground|ONE: Battleground]], where Prajanchai won the title by majority decision.{{Cite web |title=ONE Championship 'Battleground' Results: Prajanchai Dethrones Sam-A, N Sang Scores Knockout |url=https://combatpress.com/2021/07/one-championship-battleground-results/ |access-date=2021-07-30 |website=Combat Press}}\n\nA [[ONE Heavyweight web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/4/10/23677840/arjan-bhullar-vs-anatoly-malykhin-in-one-heavyweight-title-unification-bout-rescheduled-for-july-15/|title=Arjan Bhullar vs. Anatoly Malykhin in ONE heavyweight title unification bout rescheduled for July 14|website=mmafighting.com|date=10 April 2023 |access-date=2023-04-10}} The pair were previously scheduled to square off at [[ONE 161]] and then [[ONE Fight Night 8]], but were moved to this event due to a change in broadcast commitments. However, on May 19, 2023, it was announced that the fight was moved to [[2023 in ONE Championship#ONE Friday Fights 22|ONE Friday Fights 22]].{{Cite web|url=https://asianmma.com/arjan-bhullar-faces-anatoly-malykhin-at-one-friday-fights-22/|title=Arjan Bhullar faces Anatoly Malykhin at ONE Friday Fights 22|website=asianmma.com|date=20 May 2023 |access-date=2023-05-20}} \n\n== Results ==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Fight card (YouTube / Watch ONE)}}\n{{MMAevent bout|Strawweight Muay Thai|[[Prajanchai P.K.Saenchaimuaythaigym|Prajanchai P.K.Saenchai]]|def.|[[Sam-A Gaiyanghadao]]|KO (elbow)|2|2:10|For the interim [[List of ONE Championship champions#Strawweight World Championship|ONE Strawweight Muay Thai World Championship]].\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout|Flyweight Muay Thai|[[Superlek Kiatmuu9]]|def.|[[Nabil Anane]]|KO (straight to the body)|1|2:03|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout|Catchweight (142 lbs) Muay Thai|[[Saeksan Or. event\n|name=ONE Friday Fights 8: Petsukumvit vs. Petchmuangsri\n|image=\n|image_size=\n|promotion=[[ONE Championship]]\n|date={{Start date|2023|03|10}}\n|venue=[[Lumpinee Boxing Sinsamut 2]]\n}}\n\n'''''ONE Friday Fights 8: Petsukumvit vs. Petchmuangsri''''' (also known as '''''ONE Lumpinee 8''''') was a [[combat sport]]s event produced by [[ONE Championship]] that took place on March 10, 2023, at [[Lumpinee Boxing Stadium]] in [[Bangkok]], Thailand.\n\n=== Background ===\nA flyweight muay thai bout between [[Phetsukumvit Boybangna]] and Petchmuangsri Aodtukdaeng served as the event headliner.\n\n=== Bonus awards ===\nThe following fighters received $10,000 bonuses.\n*'''Performance of the Night: Petsukumvit Boibangna, Petlampun Muadablampang, Numsurin Chor.Ketwina, Banluerit Or.Atchariya, Maisangkum Sor. Yingcharoenkarnchang''' and '''Jomhod AutoMuayThai'''\n\n===Results===\n{| class=\"wikitable\" style=\"font-size: 80%;\"\n|- \n! colspan=\"8\" style=\"background-color: #ccf; color: #000080; Lumpinee 11''''') was a [[combat sport]]s event produced by [[ONE Championship]] that took place on March 31, 2023, at [[Lumpinee Boxing Stadium]] in [[Bangkok]], Thailand.\n\n=== Background ===\nA 130 pounds catchweight muay thai bout between [[Superball Tded99]] and [[Kongklai AnnyMuayThai]] served as the event headliner.\n\n=== Bonus awards ===\nThe following fighters received $10,000 \n|image=\n|image_size=\n|promotion=[[ONE Championship]]\n|date={{Start date|2023|04|7}}\n|venue=[[Lumpinee Boxing Stadium]]\n|city=[[Bangkok]], Thailand \n|previousevent=[[#ONE Friday Fights 11|ONE (also known as '''''ONE Lumpinee 12''''') was a [[combat sport]]s event produced by [[ONE Championship]] that took place on April 7, 2023, at [[Lumpinee Boxing Stadium]] in [[Bangkok]], Thailand.\n\n=== Background ===\nA flyweight muay thai bout between [[Phetsukumvit Boybangna]] and [[Kongthoranee Sor.Sommai]] served as the event headliner.\n\n=== Bonus awards ===\nThe following fighters received $10,000 bonuses.\n*'''Performance of the Night: Dentungtong Singha Mawynn, Tubtimthong Sor.Jor.Lekmuangnon, Zeta Chor.Chokamnuay''' and '''Samingdam Looksuan'''\n\n===Results===\n{| headliner.\n\n=== Bonus awards ===\nThe following fighters received $10,000 bonuses.\n*'''Performance of the Night: Soner \u015een, ET Tded99, Jack Jackmuaythai Gym, Nongchamp Luckybantermg, Amnuaydet [[combat sport]]s event produced by [[ONE Championship]] that took place on December 15, 2023, at [[Lumpinee Boxing Stadium]] in [[Bangkok]], Thailand.\n\n=== Background ===\n\nA bantamweight muay thai bout between Otop Or.Kwanmuang and Soner \u015een served as the event headliner.\n\n=== Bonus awards ===\nThe following fighters received $10,000 bonuses.\n*'''Performance of the Night: Soner The two fighters will be participating in the tournament alongside includes [[Superbon Singha Mawynn]] and [[Chingiz Allazov]].{{Cite web |date=2024-04-12 |title=ONE Championship to Hold Featherweight Kickboxing Grand Prix in 2024 Featuring Chingiz Allazov & Superbon |url=https://beyondkick.com/news/one-championship-to-hold-featherweight-kickboxing-grand-prix-in-2024-featuring-chingiz-allazov-and-superbon/ |access-date=2024-05-21 |website=Beyond Kickboxing |language=en-US}}\n\n==ONE Friday Fights 47==\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=ONE Friday Fights 47: Suakim vs. Balyko\n|image=\n|image_size=320\n|promotion=[[ONE Championship]]\n|date={{Start date|2024|01|12}}\n|venue=[[Lumpinee Boxing Stadium]]\n|city=[[Bangkok]], Thailand \n|previousevent=[[ONE Friday Fights 46|ONE Friday Fights 46: Tawanchai vs. Superbon]] \n|followingevent=[[ONE Fight Night 18|ONE Fight Night 18: Gasanov Vs. Oh]] \n}}\n\n'''''ONE Friday Fights 47: Suakim vs. Balyko''''' (also known as '''''ONE Lumpinee 47''''') was a [[combat sport]]s event produced by [[ONE Championship]] that took place on January 12, 2024, at [[Lumpinee Boxing Stadium]] in [[Bangkok]], Thailand.\n\n=== Background ===\n\nA 140-pound catchweight muay thai bout between former three-division [[Lumpinee Stadium]] world champion [[Suakim PK Saenchaimuaythaigym|Suakim Sor.Jor.Tongprajin]] and Alexey Balyko served as the event headliner.\n\n=== Bonus awards ===\nThe following fighters received $10,000 bonuses.\n*'''Performance of the Night: Alexey Balyko, Kompetch Fairtex, Khunsuk Sor.Dechapan, Numpangna EagleMuayThai''' and '''Apiwat Sor.Somnuk'''\n\n===Results===\n{| class=\"wikitable\" style=\"font-size: 80%;\"\n|- \n! 9, 2024, at [[Lumpinee Boxing Stadium]] in [[Bangkok]], Thailand.\n\n=== Background ===\n\nA bantamweight muay thai bout between former Super Lightweight (140 lbs) Channel 7 Stadium champion [[Rambolek Chor.Ajalaboon]] and Soner \u015een served as the event headliner.\n\nThe headline international bout, Mavlud Tupiev was expected to face [[Avatar Tor.Morsri|Avatar P.K.Saenchai]] in a bantamweight muay thai bout, but he withdrew due to illness and was replaced by Antar Kacem.\n\n=== Bonus awards ===\nThe following fighters received $10,000 bonuses.\n*'''Performance of the Night: sport]]s event produced by [[ONE Championship]] that took place on March 22, 2024, at [[Lumpinee Boxing Stadium]] in [[Bangkok]], Thailand.\n\n=== Background ===\n\nA bantamweight muay thai bout between [[Ferrari Fairtex]] and Mavlud Tupiev served as the event headliner.\n\n=== Bonus awards ===\nThe following fighters received $10,000 bonuses.\n*'''Performance of the Night: Jelte Blommaert, Tonglampoon FA.Group, Prakaipetchlek EminentAir, Furkan Karaba\u011f, Rak Erawan, Rifdean Masdor, Sonrak Fairtex''' and '''Kaenlek Sor.Chokmeechai'''\n\n===Results===\n{| class=\"wikitable\" 57: Panrit vs. Balyko\n|image=\n|image_size=320\n|promotion=[[ONE Championship]]\n|date={{Start date|2024|03|29}}\n|venue=[[Lumpinee Boxing Stadium]]\n|city=[[Bangkok]], Thailand Fights 57: Panrit vs. Balyko''''' (also known as '''''ONE Lumpinee 57''''') is a [[combat sport]]s event produced by [[ONE Championship]] that took place on March 29, 2024, at [[Lumpinee Boxing Stadium]] in [[Bangkok]], Thailand.\n\n=== Background ===\n\nA 140-pounds muay thai bout between Panrit Lukjaomaesaiwaree and Alexey Balyko served as the event headliner.\n\nThant Zin was expected to face Tai SorJor.Piek-U-Thai in a 136 pounds muay thai bout, but the bout was cancelled due to he illness and have symptoms of [[Appendicitis]].\n\n=== Bonus awards ===\nThe following fighters received $10,000 bonuses.\n*'''Performance of the Night: Sungprab Lukpichit, Parham Gheirati, Moe Htet Aung''' and '''Tuan Tran'''\n\n===Results===\n{| class=\"wikitable\" style=\"font-size: 80%;\"\n|- \n! \n| \n|-\n|}\n\n==ONE Friday Fights 58==\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=ONE Friday Fights 58: Superbon vs. Grigorian 3\n|image=\n|image_size=320\n|promotion=[[ONE Championship]]\n|date={{Start date|2024|04|5}}\n|venue=[[Lumpinee Boxing Stadium]]\n|city=[[Bangkok]], Thailand \n|previousevent=[[ONE Friday Fights 57|ONE Friday Fights 57: Panrit vs. Balyko]] \n|followingevent=[[ONE Fight Night 21|ONE Fight Night 21: Eersel vs. Nicolas]] \n}}\n\n'''''ONE Friday Fights 58: Superbon vs. Grigorian 3''''' (also known as '''''ONE Lumpinee 58''''') is a [[combat sport]]s event produced by [[ONE Championship]] that took place on April 5, 2024, at [[Lumpinee Boxing Stadium]] in [[Bangkok]], Thailand.\n\n=== Background ===\n\nA trilogy bout for the interim [[ONE Featherweight Kickboxing World Championship]] between former champion [[Superbon Singha Mawynn]] and former [[Glory (kickboxing)#Glory Lightweight Championship|Glory Lightweight]] champion [[Marat Grigorian]] headlined the event. The pairing first met at [[2018 in Kunlun Fight#Kunlun Fight 69|Kunlun Fight 69]] on February 4, 2018, where Grigorian won via knockout in the first-round. Their second meeting took place at [[ONE: X]] on March 25, 2022, which ended in a unanimous decision in favor of Superbon.\n\nA [[ONE Strawweight Kickboxing World Championship]] bout between reigning champion [[Jonathan Di Bella]] and reigning [[ONE Strawweight Muay Thai World Championship|ONE Strawweight Muay Thai]] World champion [[Prajanchai P.K.Saenchaimuaythaigym|Prajanchai P.K.Saenchai]] was expected to serve as co-main event. However, as Di Bella was unable to reach the required hydration levels at the weigh-ins, the fight was cancelled and Di Bella was stripped of his title.\n\n=== Bonus awards ===\nThe following fighters received $20,000 bonuses.\n*'''Performance of the Night: Shadow Singmawynn'''\nThe following fighters received $10,000 bonuses.\n*'''Performance of the Night: Nakrob Fairtex''' and '''Muangthai P.K.Saenchai'''\n\n===Results===\n{| class=\"wikitable\" style=\"font-size: 80%;\"\n|- \n! colspan=\"8\" style=\"background-color: #ccf; color: #000080; text-align: center;\" | '''ONE Friday Fights 58 (PPV at Watch ONE)'''\n|-\n! colspan=\"1\" style=\"background-color: #E6E8FA; color: #000000; text-align: center;\" | Weight Class\n! colspan=\"1\" style=\"background-color: #E6E8FA; color: #000000; text-align: center;\" | \n! colspan=\"1\" style=\"background-color: #E6E8FA; color: #000000; text-align: center;\" | \n! colspan=\"1\" style=\"background-color: #E6E8FA; color: #000000; text-align: center;\" | \n! colspan=\"1\" style=\"background-color: #E6E8FA; color: #000000; text-align: center;\" | Method\n! colspan=\"1\" style=\"background-color: #E6E8FA; color: #000000; text-align: center;\" | Round\n! colspan=\"1\" style=\"background-color: #E6E8FA; color: #000000; text-align: center;\" | Time\n! colspan=\"1\" style=\"background-color: #E6E8FA; color: #000000; text-align: center;\" | Notes\n|-\n|Featherweight Kickboxing\n|{{flagicon|THA}} [[Superbon Singha Mawynn]] \n|def.\n|{{flagicon|ARM}} [[Marat Grigorian]] \n|Decision (unanimous) \n|align=center| 5 \n|align=center| 3:00 \n|For the interim [[ONE Featherweight Kickboxing World Championship]]. \n|-\n|Bantamweight Muay Thai\n|{{flagicon|THA}} [[Nong-O Gaiyanghadao|Nong-O Hama]]\n|def.\n|{{flagicon|THA}} [[Kulabdam Sor.Jor.Piek-U-Thai]] \n|Decision (unanimous) \n|align=center| 3 \n|align=center| 3:00 \n| \n|-\n|Catchweight (142 lbs) Muay Thai\n|{{flagicon|JPN}} Yutaro Asahi \n|def.\n|{{flagicon|THA}} [[Saeksan Or. event\n|name=ONE Friday Fights 62: Mongkolkaew vs. ET 2\n|image=\n|image_size=320\n|promotion=[[ONE Championship]]\n|date={{Start date|2024|05|10}}\n|venue=[[Lumpinee Boxing May 10, 2024, at [[Lumpinee Boxing Stadium]] in [[Bangkok]], Thailand.\n\n=== Background ===\nA featherweight muay thai bout between the 2022 RWS welterweight winner [[Shadow Singmawynn|Shadow Singha Mawynn]] and former [[ONE Featherweight Muay Thai World Championship]] challenger [[Jimmy Vienot]] was expected to headline the event. However, Shadow withdrew due to illness and the bout was scrapped.\n\nA 130-pounds catchweight muay thai bout between Mongkolkaew Sor.Sommai and ET Wankhongohm MBK headlined the event.\n\nFurkan Karaba\u011f was expected to face Mavlonbek Kakhkhorov in a featherweight muay thai bout at headline international card, but he pulled out in a last minute due to illness. The headlined of the international card was promoted a 127-pounds catchweight muay thai between Wei Ziqin vs. Riamu Matsumoto instead.\n\n=== Bonus awards ===\nThe following fighters received $10,000 bonuses.\n*'''Performance of the Night: Chokpreecha P.K.Saenchai, Kongkula Jitmuangon''' and '''Yuki Morioka'''\n\n===Results===\n{| class=\"wikitable\" style=\"font-size: 80%;\"\n|- \n! colspan=\"8\" As a results, a bantamweight muay thai bout between Parham Gheirati and Jordan Godtfredsen promoted to main event status.\n\n=== Bonus awards ===\nThe following fighters received $10,000 Fights 65==\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=ONE Friday Fights 65: Jaosuayai vs. Puengluang\n|image=\n|image_size=320\n|promotion=[[ONE Championship]]\n|date={{Start date|2024|05|31}}\n|venue=[[Lumpinee Kongchai vs. Hamidi]] \n}}\n\n'''''ONE Friday Fights 65: Jaosuayai vs. Puengluang''''' (also known as '''''ONE Lumpinee 65''''') was a [[combat sport]]s event produced by [[ONE Championship]] that took place on May 31, 2024, at [[Lumpinee Boxing Stadium]] in [[Bangkok]], Thailand.\n\n=== Background ===\nA flyweight muay thai bout between [[Jawsuayai Sor.Dechaphan|Jaosuayai Mor.Krungthepthonburi]] and Puengluang Baanramba headlined the event.{{Cite web |date=2024-05-27 |title=Full Card Revealed for ONE Friday Fights 65 on May 31 \n|url=https://www.onefc.com/news/full-card-revealed-for-one-friday-fights-65-on-may-31/ |access-date=2024-07-17 |website=ONE Championship \u2013 The Home Of Martial Arts |language=en-US}}\n\nIlyas Musaev was expected to rematch against Yod-IQ Or.Primonsri at a headlined international card, but he withdrew due to injury and the bout was scrapped. Instead, the promotion was promoted a 147-pounds muay thai between [[Avatar Tor.Morsri|Avatar P.K.Saenchai]] and Abdulla Dayakaev to new main event international card.\n\n=== Bonus awards ===\nThe following fighters received $10,000 bonuses.\n*'''Performance of the Night: Jaosuayai Mor.Krungthepthonburi, Watcharaphon P.K.Saenchai, Tahaneak Nayokathasala, Samransing Sitchalongsak''' and '''Carlo Bumina-ang'''\n\n===Results===\n{| class=\"wikitable\" style=\"font-size: 80%;\"\n|- \n! colspan=\"8\" style=\"background-color: #ccf; color: #000080; text-align: center;\" | '''ONE Friday Fights 65 choke)\n|align=center|2\n|align=center|4:03\n|\n|}\n\n==ONE Friday Fights 90==\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=ONE Friday Fights 90: Kongklai vs. Kacem\n|image=\n|image_size=320\n|promotion=[[ONE Kacem''''' (also known as '''''ONE Lumpinee 90''''') was a [[combat sport]]s event produced by [[ONE Championship]] that took place on December 6, 2024, at [[Lumpinee Boxing Stadium]] in [[Bangkok]], Thailand.\n\n===Background===\nThe event was headlined by a 142-pounds muay thai bout between [[Kongklai Sor.Sommai]] and Antar Kacem.{{Cite web |date=2024-12-02 |title=Full Card Revealed for ONE Arts |language=en-US}}\n\n===Bonus awards===\nThe following fighters received $10,000 bonuses:\n*'''Performance of the Night: Antar Kacem, Asadula Imangazaliev, Xavier Gonzalez, Alessio Malatesta''' and '''Ubaid Hussain'''\n\n===Results===\n{| class=\"wikitable\" style=\"font-size: 80%;\"\n|- \n! colspan=\"8\" style=\"background-color: #ccf; color: #000080; text-align: center;\" | '''ONE Friday event\n|name=ONE Friday Fights 93: Kongchai vs. Ondash\n|image=\n|image_size=320\n|promotion=[[ONE Championship]]\n|date={{start date|2025|01|10}}\n|venue=[[Lumpinee Boxing Stadium]]\n|city=[[Bangkok]], Thailand \n|previousevent=\n|followingevent=\n}}\n'''''ONE Friday Fights 93: Kongchai vs. Ondash''''' (also known as '''''ONE Lumpinee 93''''') was a [[combat sport]]s event produced by [[ONE Championship]] that took place on January 10, 2025, at [[Lumpinee Boxing Stadium]] in [[Bangkok]], Thailand.\n\n===Background===\nA strawweight Muay Thai bout between Por.Borirak]] was headlined the event.{{Cite web |date=2025-09-08 |title=Full Card Revealed for ONE Friday Fights 124 on September Liu''''' (also known as '''''ONE Lumpinee 137''''') was a [[combat sport]]s event produced by [[ONE Championship]] that took place on December 19, 2025, at [[Lumpinee Boxing Stadium]] in [[Bangkok]], Thailand.\n\n===background===\nA featherweight Kickboxing bout between current [[ONE Featherweight Muay Thai World Championship|ONE Featherweight Muay Thai World Champion]] [[Tawanchai P.K. Saenchaimuaythaigym|Tawanchai P.K.Saenchai]] and Liu Mengyang was headlined the event.{{Cite web |date=2025-12-14 |title=Full Card Revealed for ONE Friday Fights 137 on December 19\n|url=https://www.onefc.com/news/full-card-revealed-for-one-friday-fights-137-on-december-19/ |access-date=2025-12-15 |website=ONE Championship \u2013 The Home Of Martial Arts |language=en-US}}\n\n===Bonus awards===\nThe following fighters received $10,000 bonuses:\n*'''Performance of the Night: Sam-A Gaiyanghadao, Mohammad Siasarani, Yod-IQ Or.Pimonsri, Gingsanglek Tor.Laksong, Suriyanlek Por.Yenying''' and '''Duangdawnoi Looksaikongdin'''\n\n===Results===\n{| class=\"wikitable\" style=\"font-size: 80%;\"\n|- \n! colspan=\"8\" style=\"background-color: #ccf; color: #000080; text-align: center;\" | '''ONE Friday Fights 137 (YouTube / Watch ONE)'''\n|-\n! colspan=\"1\" style=\"background-color: #E6E8FA; color: #000000; text-align: center;\" | Weight Class\n! colspan=\"1\" style=\"background-color: #E6E8FA; color: #000000; text-align: center;\" | \n! colspan=\"1\" style=\"background-color: #E6E8FA; color: #000000; text-align: center;\" | \n! colspan=\"1\" style=\"background-color: #E6E8FA; color: #000000; text-align: center;\" | \n! colspan=\"1\" style=\"background-color: #E6E8FA; color: #000000; text-align: center;\" | Method\n! colspan=\"1\" style=\"background-color: #E6E8FA; color: #000000; text-align: center;\" | Round\n! colspan=\"1\" style=\"background-color: #E6E8FA; color: #000000; text-align: center;\" | Time\n! colspan=\"1\" style=\"background-color: #E6E8FA; color: #000000; text-align: center;\" | Notes\n|-\n|Featherweight Kickboxing\n|{{flagicon|CHN}} Liu Mengyang\n|def.\n|{{flagicon|THA}} [[Tawanchai P.K.
